## 1. Purpose / Scope

This catalog is the single cross-domain register of API error codes for the Phase 6 surface. It fixes, for every active code, the canonical HTTP status, visibility, retryable value and detail contract, and it maps the error families that apply to each of the 369 AMI REST routes, the 4 WebSocket routes, the 5 callbacks and the 4 internal routes.

In scope: public REST, platform-admin REST, internal API, callback and WebSocket error semantics. Out of scope: API versioning, new endpoints, database changes, and any change to the source contracts. Nothing in this catalog introduces a new code, status or detail field.

## 2. Authority / Source Precedence

| Rank | Authority | Governs |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | 6A §24 (L635–L661), 6A status table (L138–L148) | Envelope shape, global codes, status meanings, retryable boolean |
| 2 | Domain owner error tables 6B–6M | Domain codes, statuses, applicability |
| 3 | AAM (principal / credential / cross-tenant columns; legend L108–L127, L354–L375, L574, L593) | Authentication and authorization outcomes per route |
| 4 | AMI (L117 onward; WS L551–L554) | Route inventory: 369 REST + 4 WS |
| 5 | FAR | Cross-document reconciliations; where FAR is broader than 6B/AAM on the 401 family, 6B/AAM win (AEC-F-02) |

Route profiles used in §15: TENANT-KEY (user access token or API key), TENANT-SESSION (user access token only), PLATFORM-ADMIN (PA gate), INTERNAL (internal-service principal), CALLBACK (verification profile), PRE-AUTH / RESTRICTED (anonymous or restricted token). Profiles derive from the AAM principal/credential column.

## 3. Error Envelope

| Field | Rule | Source |
|---|---|---|
| code | Machine-readable, stable UPPER_SNAKE_CASE; clients branch on it, never on message | 6A L653 |
| message | Human-readable, safe to display; never contains internals | 6A L654 |
| details | Structured, error-family-specific; see §6 | 6A L655 |
| request_id | Matches the request correlation ID | 6A L656 |
| retryable | Explicit boolean; never a string or tri-state on the wire | 6A L657 |

Callback response semantics are route-specific; see §12. The provider media WebSocket carries no generic envelope (§13).

## 4. Error Taxonomy

| Category | Name | Count | Wire treatment |
|---|---|---|---|
| A | ACTIVE_PUBLIC_REST_ERROR | 102 | active — public envelope |
| B | ACTIVE_PLATFORM_ADMIN_ERROR | 24 | active — platform-admin envelope |
| C | ACTIVE_INTERNAL_API_ERROR | 3 | active — internal API only |
| D | CALLBACK_FAILURE | 3 | active — callback internal outcome |
| E | WEBSOCKET_CLOSE_OR_ERROR_EVENT | 0 | none (WS uses close codes, §13) |
| F | DETAILS_REASON_ONLY | 54 | historical/detail-discriminator lexical bucket (exceptions in §6.1/§6.2) |
| G | INTERNAL_NORMALIZATION_CLASS | 6 | internal — never on wire |
| H | INTERNAL_DOMAIN_FAILURE | 10 | internal — never on wire |
| I | RESERVED_FUTURE | 5 | reserved — not emitted |
| J | SUPERSEDED_OR_DEPRECATED | 1 | superseded — not emitted |
| K | EXPLICITLY_NOT_INTRODUCED | 18 | not introduced — never emitted |
| L | NON_ERROR_SUCCESS_OR_SIGNAL | 22 | non-error (success or signal) |
| M | AUDIT_OR_EVENT_VOCABULARY | 87 | audit/event vocabulary — not an error |
| N | DB_SQLSTATE_OR_INTERNAL_SIGNAL | 9 | internal DB signal — never public |
| X | EXCLUDED_NOT_ERROR_VOCABULARY | 77 | not error vocabulary |
| Σ | Total | 421 | active A+B+C+D = 132; F includes the two AEC-OD-06 merge cap tokens; H includes the two internal-only merge not-found tokens (AEC-F-55) |

## 6. Error Details Schemas

| Code | Details contract |
|---|---|
| DEPENDENCY_UNAVAILABLE | `details.reason` ∈ {`CAPACITY_QUOTA_NOT_CONFIGURED`, `CAPACITY_AUTHORITY_UNAVAILABLE`} on AMI-6D-001 call initiation (6D L1333; 6D L2022/L2023) and AMI-6H-007 campaign start (6H L1581; 6H L2756/L2757); `COMPLIANCE_POLICY_NOT_FOUND` on AMI-6H-006/AMI-6H-007 (6H L1794); with `CAPACITY_QUOTA_NOT_CONFIGURED` on AMI-6D-001 direct outbound only, also `details.metric = "CONCURRENT_CALLS"` (6K L3797); otherwise `{}` |
| PAYMENT_FAILED | normalized FailureCode only (6K L2547); never raw provider payload |
| QUOTA_EXCEEDED | `details.metric = "CONCURRENT_CALLS"` on AMI-6D-001 direct outbound at the concurrent-call limit (6D L2021; 6K L3796; FAR L796/L1470); `details.metric = "ACTIVE_AGENTS"` plus the effective `limit` on the 6E agent-count quota, AMI-6E-001/AMI-6E-007 (6E L1669); billing hard-limit example `details = {metric: "CALL_MINUTES", hard_limit, current_usage}` (6K L3477); field registry §6.3 |
| STATE_CONFLICT | illustrative 6A L655 example (e.g.) of state-conflict detail: `{current_state, attempted_transition}` — an example, not a mandatory shape; domain-scoped `details.reason` values and any reason-specific fields only per §6.1 (Additional details fields column); normative: every guarded-transition 409 carries `details.current_state` (6A L459); `details.field = "name"` on the AMI-6F-001 concurrent duplicate Knowledge Base name (6F L264; source "409 CONFLICT" normalized, AEC-F-57); field registry §6.3 |
| VALIDATION_ERROR | illustrative 6A L655 example (e.g.) of field-level validation detail: `[{field, issue}]` — an example, not a mandatory shape; domain-scoped `details.reason` values only where the owning contract names them, per §6.1 (reason, parent, owner line and routes) |
| WEBHOOK_TOPIC_INVALID | `details.invalid_topics` — the unrecognized `topics` values on AMI-6J-018 registration and AMI-6J-020 update (6J L667; 6J L1434; owner decision AEC-OD-07); field registry §6.3 |

Details rules:

1. Route- and domain-specific details contracts in the owning source are authoritative; this catalog records them and adds none.
2. §6.3 registers every details field the sources define, with parent code, scope, classification and source lines; §6.1 and §6.1.1 register every details.reason value in exact spelling.
3. Where no owning source requires a field for a code or condition, this catalog mandates no additional reason-specific field.
4. Normative: every guarded-transition 409 carries `details.current_state` so the client can resync (6A L459).
5. The detail shapes at 6A L655 are illustrative examples only.

Rules: a `details.reason` value is valid only for the code and owner that name it; no details field is legalized globally. Raw provider payloads, stack traces, SQLSTATE and normalized failure-class names never appear in `details` (6A L654; 6J L1400).

Category F tokens (historical/detail-discriminator lexical bucket, 54): `AI_NOTE_IMMUTABLE`, `ALREADY_CONVERTED`, `ALREADY_SUPPRESSED`, `CALLING_WINDOW_CLOSED`, `CAMPAIGN_ALREADY_STARTED`, `CAMPAIGN_CONTACT_TERMINAL`, `CAMPAIGN_NOT_EDITABLE`, `CAMPAIGN_NOT_RUNNING`, `CAMPAIGN_WINDOW_EXCEEDS_ORG_POLICY`, `CAMPAIGN_WINDOW_EXHAUSTED`, `CAPACITY_AUTHORITY_UNAVAILABLE`, `CAPACITY_QUOTA_NOT_CONFIGURED`, `CHECKPOINT_UNRECOVERABLE_AFTER_SIDE_EFFECT`, `COMPLIANCE_POLICY_NOT_FOUND`, `CONCURRENCY_LIMIT_REACHED`, `CONSENT_APPEND_ONLY`, `CONSENT_NOT_VALID`, `CONTACT_ALREADY_MERGED`, `CONTACT_INELIGIBLE`, `CONTACT_LIST_NOT_READY`, `CONTACT_SUPPRESSED`, `DEAL_TERMINAL`, `DUPLICATE_CAMPAIGN_CONTACT`, `DUPLICATE_CONTENT`, `DUPLICATE_DOMAIN`, `DUPLICATE_PHONE`, `EMBEDDING_MODEL_IMMUTABLE`, `EMPTY_CONTACT_LIST`, `ILLEGAL_CAMPAIGN_TRANSITION`, `ILLEGAL_LEAD_TRANSITION`, `IMPORT_ALREADY_PROCESSING`, `INTEGRATION_BINDING_UNRESOLVED`, `INVALID_CALLING_WINDOW`, `INVALID_PIPELINE_STAGE`, `INVALID_QUALIFICATION_STATE`, `INVALID_RETRY_POLICY`, `IN_USE`, `LATEST_ATTEMPT_NOT_FAILED`, `MAX_ATTEMPTS_EXHAUSTED`, `MAX_CUSTOM_FIELDS`, `MAX_RETRY_ATTEMPTS_EXCEEDED`, `MAX_TURNS_EXCEEDED_NO_EXIT`, `MERGE_CUSTOM_FIELD_CAP_EXCEEDED`, `MERGE_PRIMARY_ALREADY_MERGED`, `MERGE_PRIMARY_ERASED`, `MERGE_SECONDARY_ALREADY_MERGED`, `MERGE_SECONDARY_ERASED`, `MERGE_SELF_REJECTED`, `MERGE_TAG_CAP_EXCEEDED`, `NUMBER_NOT_PROVISIONED`, `PIPELINE_STAGE_HAS_DEALS`, `SUPPRESSION_NOT_LIFTABLE`, `TENANT_CALL_QUOTA_REACHED`, `UNKNOWN_NODE_TYPE`.

Class F is a lexical bucket, not a claim that every F token is a details.reason: IN_USE is class F and a details.current_state value (§6.2); AGENT_NOT_PUBLISHED (A), CAMPAIGN_PAUSED (L) and ORGANIZATION_SUSPENDED (M) are §6.1 reasons outside F (AEC-F-43).

### 6.1 details.reason registry

Each `details.reason` value is valid only under its parent code and only on the routes listed. Parent and status are taken from the owner reason table or owner prose, and are route-specific where an owner decision says so (CONTACT_LIST_NOT_READY, AEC-OD-03; INVALID_PIPELINE_STAGE, AEC-OD-04; Contact merge MERGE_* reasons, one parent and status per guard, AEC-OD-06). The 6A L655 detail shapes are illustrative examples, not mandatory; the Additional details fields column names only fields the owning source requires. Rows marked internal-only, advisory or structurally unreachable are recorded for completeness and are not emitted in any client response.

## 7. HTTP Status Selection Rules

| Status | Use | Source |
|---|---|---|
| 400 | Malformed request (VALIDATION_ERROR and 400 domain codes) | 6A L138 |
| 401 | Missing, invalid, expired or revoked credential (6B §22 family) | 6A L139; 6B L2069–L2079 |
| 403 | Authenticated but not permitted, key-ineligible, TPF, break-glass | 6A L140; AAM L108/L123/L124 |
| 404 | Not found or other tenant — never distinguishes | 6A L141; AAM L574 |
| 409 | State conflict, duplicate, idempotency-key reuse mismatch; route-specific: STATE_CONFLICT with details.reason=CONTACT_LIST_NOT_READY on AMI-6H-005/006 (AEC-OD-03) and details.reason=INVALID_PIPELINE_STAGE on AMI-6G-033 (AEC-OD-04); STATE_CONFLICT with details.reason=MERGE_PRIMARY_ALREADY_MERGED, MERGE_SECONDARY_ALREADY_MERGED, MERGE_PRIMARY_ERASED or MERGE_SECONDARY_ERASED on AMI-6G-011 (AEC-OD-06); STATE_CONFLICT with details.field="name" on AMI-6F-001 duplicate Knowledge Base name (source "409 CONFLICT" normalized; AEC-F-57) | 6A L142; 6H L1776; 6G L1041; 6G L1049; 6F L264 |
| 410 | Gone (OAuth state expired only) | 6J L481 |
| 412 | If-Match precondition failed — If-Match routes only | 6A L143 |
| 413 | Payload too large | 6A L144 |
| 422 | Semantically invalid request (VALIDATION_ERROR and 422 domain codes); route-specific: VALIDATION_ERROR with details.reason=CONTACT_LIST_NOT_READY on AMI-6H-007 only (AEC-OD-03); VALIDATION_ERROR with details.reason=MERGE_SELF_REJECTED, MERGE_TAG_CAP_EXCEEDED or MERGE_CUSTOM_FIELD_CAP_EXCEEDED on AMI-6G-011 (AEC-OD-06); INVALID_QUALIFICATION_STATE and MAX_CUSTOM_FIELDS 422 only; WEBHOOK_TOPIC_INVALID with details.invalid_topics on AMI-6J-018/AMI-6J-020 (AEC-OD-07) | 6A L145; 6H L1567; 6G L1049; 6J L667; 6J L1434 |
| 429 | RATE_LIMIT_EXCEEDED with Retry-After; QUOTA_EXCEEDED commercial without Retry-After; never for the advisory CONCURRENCY_LIMIT_REACHED signal on AMI-6H-007 (AEC-OD-05) | 6A L146; 6A L544; 6D L1333; 6H L1781 |
| 500 | INTERNAL_ERROR; never leaks internals | 6A L147 |
| 502 | DEPENDENCY_UNAVAILABLE on AMI-6F-020 only, when the embedding provider was reached but returned a failed or unusable response (AEC-OD-02) | 6F L849; 6F L1046 |
| 503 | DEPENDENCY_UNAVAILABLE default; upstream / dependency unavailable | 6A L148 |

HTTP 504 is not used with DEPENDENCY_UNAVAILABLE in V1 (AEC-OD-02).

Every active code has one canonical status in §5 except the governed multi-status codes: VALIDATION_ERROR 400/422 by failure kind (AEC-F-13) and DEPENDENCY_UNAVAILABLE 503 by default, 502 on AMI-6F-020 only (AEC-OD-02). SESSION_REVOKE_NOOP and ANALYTICS_DATA_NOT_AVAILABLE are HTTP 200 non-errors (§14).

## 8. Auth / Authorization Precedence

1. Authentication: missing/invalid/expired/revoked credential → 401 family (6B L2069–L2079; AAM L122/L354). Callbacks skip this step and use their verification profile (AAM L125).
2. Principal gate: platform-admin routes require the PA gate — non-PA human → 403 PLATFORM_ADMIN_REQUIRED (6M L397); tenant principal or API key → 403 TENANT_PRINCIPAL_FORBIDDEN (6M L398; AAM L124). Internal routes accept only the internal-service principal (AAM L371/L372).
3. Tenant resolution / concealment: a resource of another tenant → 404 RESOURCE_NOT_FOUND, never 403 (AAM L574; 6A L141).
4. Key eligibility: a valid API key on a key-ineligible route → 403 AUTHORIZATION_DENIED (AAM L108/L123); key scope/ceiling → 403 (AAM L354).
5. Permission: insufficient RBAC permission → 403 AUTHORIZATION_DENIED (6A L140).
6. Break-glass (platform admin acting on tenant data): missing/failed/wrong-purpose grant → 403 BREAK_GLASS_GRANT_INVALID; grant store unavailable → 503 DEPENDENCY_UNAVAILABLE (AAM L359–L363; FAR L261).
7. Sensitive media (AMI-6D-011, AMI-6D-014): 403 AUTHORIZATION_DENIED is decided before 404 RECORDING_NOT_AVAILABLE (AAM L355).
8. Validation, precondition, idempotency, domain, rate/quota and dependency outcomes follow.

## 9. Retry / Backoff

| Code | Retryable | Basis |
|---|---|---|
| DEPENDENCY_UNAVAILABLE | conditional — true by default; false for 6B L1415 Case C | see value |
| INTEGRATION_OPERATION_FAILED | conditional — per normalized failure class (6J L1428) | see value |
| INTEGRATION_PROVIDER_UNAVAILABLE | true | 6J L1423 |
| INTEGRATION_RATE_LIMITED | true | 6J L1424 |
| PAYMENT_ALREADY_IN_PROGRESS | true | 6K L2545 (poll) |
| PAYMENT_PROVIDER_UNAVAILABLE | true | 6K L2546 |
| RATE_LIMIT_EXCEEDED | true | 6A L544 (Retry-After) |
| WEBHOOK_REPLAY_RATE_LIMITED | true | 6J L1437 |

All other active codes: retryable=false (6A L646). The wire value is always boolean; conditional entries resolve to true or false per response.

### 9.1 6M retry-cell normalization (AEC-OD-01, AEC-F-03)

Owner decision AEC-OD-01 option B (6M L389; AMI L834): retryable=true means the same operation may succeed after a transient condition clears without semantic correction by the caller. The seven 6M rows below emit retryable=false; their "Yes — …" cell text is caller remediation. SESSION_REVOKE_NOOP remains an HTTP 200 non-error (6M L421).

| Code | 6M line | Source cell | Wire retryable | Remediation |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| BREAK_GLASS_GRANT_INVALID | L399 | Yes — after obtaining/activating a valid break-glass grant (§17) | false | obtain or activate a valid break-glass grant, then send a new request |
| CPA_EXPIRE_REASON_REQUIRED | L414 | Yes — with `reason` supplied | false | supply the expiry reason, then send a new request |
| CREDIT_VALIDATION_FAILED | L404 | Yes — with corrected input | false | correct the credit input, then send a new request |
| PLAN_DEACTIVATE_REASON_REQUIRED | L407 | Yes — with `reason` supplied | false | supply the deactivation reason, then send a new request |
| PLAN_PRICE_VALIDATION_FAILED | L406 | Yes — with corrected input | false | correct the plan price, then send a new request |
| QUOTA_OVERRIDE_VALIDATION_FAILED | L425 | Yes — with corrected input | false | correct the quota-override input, then send a new request |
| TAX_RULE_COMPONENTS_INVALID | L417 | Yes — with corrected input | false | correct the tax-rule components, then send a new request |

### 9.2 Rate vs quota

| Code | HTTP | Nature | Retry-After | Retryable | Alias | Source |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| RATE_LIMIT_EXCEEDED | 429 | technical rate limit | yes | true | No | 6A L146; 6A L544 |
| QUOTA_EXCEEDED | 429 | commercial quota (REFUSED_AT_LIMIT) | no Retry-After | false | No | 6D L1328/L1333; 6K L2541; FAR L263/L573 |

## 11. Internal Failure / Normalization

- INTERNAL_ERROR is 500 and never leaks internals (6A L147).
- 6J normalizes provider failures into internal failure classes (6J L1400); the classes never appear on the wire. Unclassified failures fall to UNKNOWN (6J L1411).
- SQLSTATE values and DB exceptions are mapped to catalog codes and never published.
- Internal domain failures (category H) and DB signals (category N) are recorded, not returned.
- H vs N: a token that names a business or domain condition (a missing merge party, an amount or currency mismatch, a forbidden internal path) is class H, even when a database routine raises it, and the handler normalizes it to a catalog code; a SQLSTATE or a database-level state or return signal (a check-constraint value, a claim, lease or processing-state outcome) is class N. Neither is ever returned.
- MERGE_PRIMARY_NOT_FOUND and MERGE_SECONDARY_NOT_FOUND are class H: internal-only Contact-merge guards that normalize to 404 RESOURCE_NOT_FOUND and are never exposed (6G L1049; AEC-OD-06; AEC-F-55).

| Category | Tokens | Wire treatment |
|---|---|---|
| G INTERNAL_NORMALIZATION_CLASS | AUTH_FAILED, CONFIGURATION_INVALID, PROVIDER_RATE_LIMITED, PROVIDER_UNAVAILABLE, SCOPE_INSUFFICIENT, UNKNOWN | internal — never on wire |
| H INTERNAL_DOMAIN_FAILURE | AMOUNT_MISMATCH, CPA_RAW_FUNCTION_FORBIDDEN, CREDIT_RAW_FUNCTION_FORBIDDEN, CURRENCY_MISMATCH, GATEWAY_ERROR, MERGE_PRIMARY_NOT_FOUND, MERGE_SECONDARY_NOT_FOUND, RECONCILER_SESSION_REQUIRED, WEBHOOK_MUTATION_FORBIDDEN, WORKFLOW_NODE_EXECUTION_FAILED | internal — never on wire; MERGE_PRIMARY_NOT_FOUND / MERGE_SECONDARY_NOT_FOUND normalize to 404 RESOURCE_NOT_FOUND and are never exposed (6G L1049) |
| N DB_SQLSTATE_OR_INTERNAL_SIGNAL | CREDIT_TYPE_INVALID, NOT_CLAIMABLE_AMBIGUOUS, NOT_CLAIMABLE_CLAIMED, NOT_CLAIMABLE_CONFIRMED, NOT_CLAIMABLE_SUBMITTING, NOT_CLAIM_HOLDER, NOT_FAILABLE_AFTER_SUBMISSION, NOT_RECONCILABLE_OR_NOT_FOUND, RETRY_PENDING | internal DB signal — never public |

## 12. Callback Failure Semantics

Provider callback verification is not bearer authentication. Each callback has its own contract; the table records every field, and SOURCE-UNSPECIFIED marks a field the source does not define (nothing is invented).

| Callback ID / AMI ID | Method + Path | Verification mechanism | Success response model | Error response model | Standard error envelope | Redirect behavior | Invalid / forged request behavior | Receipt persistence | Dedup / replay semantics | ACK / retry behavior | Source |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| CB-1 / AMI-6B-013 | GET /api/v1/auth/oauth/{provider}/callback | OAuth authorization code exchanged server-side with the IdP (the code is the credential) plus state match; not bearer authentication (6B L1518; 6B L1528) | same three-shape contract as §21.1: direct success / organization selection / MFA challenge (6B L1527) | 401 INVALID_CREDENTIALS-equivalent (code exchange failed or state mismatch); 403 ACCOUNT_SUSPENDED (6B L1528) | YES — standard error envelope (§3) for the 401/403 outcomes (6B L1527/L1528; 6A L653–L657) | none specified in source; the response is the §21.1 contract (6B L1527) | state mismatch or failed code exchange → 401 INVALID_CREDENTIALS-equivalent (6B L1528) | not applicable — user-initiated OAuth hop, not a provider event; no inbound receipt specified in source | code is single-use by OAuth2 design; a retried callback with the same code fails at the IdP exchange and is passed through as 401 (6B L1530) | not applicable (no provider ACK); 20/hour/IP (6B L1529) | 6B L1515–L1530 |
| CB-2 / AMI-6D-021 | POST /webhooks/voice/{provider_slug}/events | provider-native signature (HMAC or provider-specific header) verified per adapter in the Telephony ACL; not tenant JWT or API key (6D L404) | SOURCE-UNSPECIFIED (AEC-F-15) | SOURCE-UNSPECIFIED (AEC-F-15) | SOURCE-UNSPECIFIED | none — provider-facing only (6D L404) | SOURCE-UNSPECIFIED beyond per-adapter signature verification (6D L404) | durable inbound event receipt, exactly as 6A §28.2 specifies for every inbound provider callback (6D L404; 6A L776) | idempotent on the unique key (organization, provider_slug, provider_event_id) (6D L404; 6A L776) | fast accept-and-enqueue: RECEIVED → PROCESSING → PROCESSED / FAILED / SKIPPED; ACK status not specified in source (6A L776) | 6D L404; 6A L776 |
| CB-3 / AMI-6J-011 | GET /api/v1/integrations/oauth/{definition_key}/callback | browser-redirect endpoint, not a JSON API; unguessable, single-use, provider-bound state (10-minute TTL) plus authorization code; not bearer authentication (6J L476–L489; 6J L495) | HTTP 302 to {web app origin}/integrations/callback?connection_id=…&result=success, never a JSON body (6J L498) | 400 OAUTH_PROVIDER_MISMATCH (state untouched), 400 OAUTH_STATE_INVALID, 409 OAUTH_STATE_ALREADY_USED, 410 OAUTH_STATE_EXPIRED decided internally and delivered as a 302 with result=failed (6J L478–L482; 6J L498) | NO — redirect, never a JSON body (6J L498) | HTTP 302 to the platform-owned callback URL with result=success or result=failed (6J L498) | unknown state → OAUTH_STATE_INVALID; provider/definition mismatch → OAUTH_PROVIDER_MISMATCH before any state change; provider error parameter → state marked failed (6J L478; 6J L479; 6J L503) | no inbound receipt; the single-use OAuth state record transitions (6J L480; 6J L503) | state is single-use under a row lock; a replay → OAUTH_STATE_ALREADY_USED and never learns the organization (6J L480; 6J L489) | not applicable (browser redirect); per-source-IP edge ceiling, not the tenant quota (6J L497) | 6J L476–L503 |
| CB-4 / AMI-6J-014 | POST /api/v1/integrations/providers/{provider_slug}/callbacks/{opaque_connection_route_id} | opaque route id selects the candidate connection; that connection's provider-specific verification material verifies the request; tenant context trusted only after verification; not bearer authentication (6J L980–L985) | 2xx fast-ACK in the provider-specific minimal shape (6J L965; 6J L989) | verification failure → 401-equivalent or silent drop after fast-ACK (6J L986); body over 1 MB → 413 PAYLOAD_TOO_LARGE (6J L966) | NO — never the platform {data, meta} / {error} envelope (6J L989) | none | rejected (401-equivalent or silent drop) and never processed (6J L986) | durable inbound event receipt committed before the ACK; a malformed payload that passes verification is recorded FAILED (6J L965; 6J L967) | dedup key (§24.3) absorbs provider retries transparently; each event processed once (6J L968) | ACK immediately after the dedup insert commits (6J L965); the platform never asks the provider to retry (6J L968); FAILED is terminal (6J L969); layered edge rate limits (6J L988) | 6J L963–L989 |
| CB-5 / AMI-6K-023 | POST /api/v1/billing/payment-providers/{provider_slug}/webhook | verify_webhook against a platform-held webhook secret, never a tenant-configured secret; not bearer authentication (6K L2356) | fast-ACK with asynchronous processing; response body not specified in source (6K L2499) | SOURCE-UNSPECIFIED on the wire; recorded internal outcomes PAYMENT_FAILED, PAYMENT_AMOUNT_MISMATCH, PAYMENT_CURRENCY_MISMATCH, UNKNOWN_TRANSACTION_CORRELATION (6K L2547–L2550) | SOURCE-UNSPECIFIED (AEC-F-39) | none | unverified or forged webhook discarded and logged; no receipt and no domain command (6K L2356; 6K L2662) | durable payment webhook receipt, recorded only after verification (6K L2251; 6K L2356) | unique (payment provider, provider_event_id); a duplicate of a processed receipt is a no-op (6K L2401; 6K L2661) | fast-ACK, async processing (6K L2499); edge per-IP then per-provider ceiling, tenant quota never applies (6K L2357) | 6K L2251; 6K L2355–L2357; 6K L2401; 6K L2499; 6K L2547–L2550; 6K L2661–L2662 |

Callbacks carry no bearer or API-key authentication; any bearer header is ignored (AAM L125). Callback response semantics are route-specific (AEC-F-09, AEC-F-29). Retry of a failed delivery is owned by the provider.

## 13. WebSocket Error / Close Semantics

- WebSocket routes use close codes, not API error codes; category E is empty.
- 4404: cross-tenant call_id on the single-call channel, never a distinguishing error (6D L543).
- 4408: heartbeat timeout — no pong within 45s (6D L613).
- The provider media stream uses raw binary/control frames with no generic envelope (6D L405/L529).
- Tenant channels use the 6A §27.3 generic event envelope (6D L530/L531; 6I L894).

## 15. Endpoint Error Coverage — 369 routes

Global families on every non-callback route: 400/422 VALIDATION_ERROR, 429 RATE_LIMIT_EXCEEDED, 503 DEPENDENCY_UNAVAILABLE (default; 502 on AMI-6F-020 only, AEC-OD-02), 500 INTERNAL_ERROR. details.reason values appear only on the routes listed in §6.1 and §6.1.1. 413 on POST/PUT/PATCH; 412 only on If-Match routes; 409 IDEMPOTENCY_KEY_REUSE_MISMATCH only on key-bearing non-GET routes. Domain codes are attached only from route-specific evidence or explicit owner applicability.

## 18. Security Invariants

1. Cross-tenant access returns 404 RESOURCE_NOT_FOUND, never 403 (AAM L574).
2. A valid API key on a key-ineligible route returns 403 AUTHORIZATION_DENIED, not 401 (AAM L108).
3. Sensitive media: 403 before 404 RECORDING_NOT_AVAILABLE (AAM L355).
4. Callbacks never accept bearer credentials; response semantics are route-specific (AAM L125; §12).
5. INTERNAL_ERROR never leaks internals; SQLSTATE, failure classes and raw provider detail are never published.
6. BREAK_GLASS_GRANT_REQUIRED is never emitted; BREAK_GLASS_GRANT_INVALID is the only break-glass denial.
7. QUOTA_EXCEEDED never carries Retry-After and is never retryable.
8. GET routes never return IDEMPOTENCY_KEY_REUSE_MISMATCH; PRECONDITION_FAILED appears only on If-Match routes.
9. WebSocket cross-tenant subscription closes with 4404 without a distinguishing error.

## 19. Implementation Guidance

- Emit codes only from §5; map any other internal outcome to a §5 code before responding.
- Set `retryable` from §5 (conditional entries per response); never infer it from status alone.
- Populate `details` only per §6 (§6.1, §6.1.1, §6.3); every guarded-transition 409 carries `details.current_state` (6A L459); where no owning source defines a field, send `{}`.
- Apply §8 in order; concealment (404) is decided before permission (403) for foreign resources.
- Add Retry-After to RATE_LIMIT_EXCEEDED only.
- Callback handlers follow their §12 profile; response shape is route-specific and outcomes are recorded internally.
